Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in formal_languages theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 90 and the invariant I(A,B) = 885 mod 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the formal_languages decomposition. By minimality, the element a^90 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 885-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Statement: Every entropy bound deformation of the formal_languages object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 149. The moduli space has dimension at most 724 with 1 connected components.

Theorem 216.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on formal_languages structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 671. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 861 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on formal_languages structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 671. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 861. QED.
